1) 1. A web server log file is stored in HDFS. Each record contains the IP address, timestamp,
request type, URL, and HTTP status code. Write a Spark program to:
▪ Read the server log file from HDFS into an RDD.
▪ Count the total number of log entries.
▪ Extract all HTTP status codes and count their occurrences.
▪ Identify the top 5 most frequent IP addresses accessing the server.
▪ Save the status code summary to HDFS.

bdalab@computinglab23-05:~/Documents/240968040/week 8$ hdfs dfs -put server_logs.txt /user/240968040/lab8
bdalab@computinglab23-05:~/Documents/240968040/week 8$ 

q1.py:

from pyspark import SparkContext

sc = SparkContext("local","Server Log Analysis")

hdfs_input_path = "hdfs://localhost:9000/user/240968040/lab8/server_logs.txt"
hdfs_output_path = "hdfs://localhost:9000/user/240968040/lab8/output/q1/"

log_rdd = sc.textFile(hdfs_input_path)

total_entries = log_rdd.count()

parsed_rdd = log_rdd.map(lambda line:line.split(","))

status_counts = parsed_rdd.map(lambda fields:(fields[4],1)).reduceByKey(lambda a,b:a+b)

top_ips = parsed_rdd.map(lambda fields: (fields[0],1)).reduceByKey(lambda a,b:a+b).sortBy(lambda x:x[1], ascending=False).take(5)

status_counts.saveAsTextFile(hdfs_output_path)
sc.stop()


2) 2. A JSON file containing user information is stored in HDFS. Each record contains: UserID,
Name, Age, City, Occupation. Write a Spark program to:
▪ Read the JSON file into a Spark DataFrame.
▪ Display only the fields Name, Age, and City.
▪ Find all users whose age is greater than 30.
▪ Group users by city and count the number of users in each city.
▪ Identify the city having the maximum number of users.
▪ Save the filtered records to HDFS in JSON format.

q2.py:

from pyspark.sql import SparkSession
from pyspark.sql.functions import col

spark = SparkSession.builder.appName("User Data Analysis").getOrCreate()

hdfs_input_path = "hdfs://localhost:9000/user/240968040/lab8/users.json"
hdfs_output_path = "hdfs://localhost:9000/user/240968040/lab8/output/q2/"

df = spark.read.json(hdfs_input_path)

df.select("Name", "Age", "City").show()

filtered_df = df.filter(col("Age") > 30)
filtered_df.show()


city_counts = df.groupBy("City").count().withColumnRenamed("count", "UserCount")
city_counts.show()

max_city = city_counts.orderBy("UserCount", ascending=False).first()
print(f"Max city: {max_city['City']} ({max_city['UserCount']} users)")


filtered_df.write.mode("overwrite").json(hdfs_output_path)

spark.stop()

3) . A sales dataset stored in CSV format contains: TransactionID, ProductID, ProductName, Cat-
egory, Quantity, Price. Write a Spark application to:
▪ Load the CSV file from HDFS into a DataFrame.
▪ Create a new column named Revenue = Quantity × Price.
▪ Find the top 3 products generating the highest revenue.
▪ Filter transactions where revenue exceeds ₹10,000.
▪ Compute the total revenue generated by each category.
▪ Determine the category contributing the highest percentage of overall revenue.
▪ Save the transformed dataset back to HDFS in CSV format.

from pyspark.sql import SparkSession
from pyspark.sql.functions import col

spark = SparkSession.builder.appName("Sales Data Analysis").getOrCreate()

hdfs_input_path = "hdfs://localhost:9000/user/240968040/lab8/sales.csv"
hdfs_output_path = "hdfs://localhost:9000/user/240968040/lab8/output/q3/"

df = spark.read.option("header", "true").option("inferSchema", "true").csv(hdfs_input_path)
df.show()

df = df.withColumn("Revenue", col("Quantity") * col("Price"))


df.groupBy("ProductID", "ProductName").sum("Revenue").withColumnRenamed("sum(Revenue)", "TotalRevenue").orderBy("TotalRevenue", ascending=False).show(3, truncate=False)

filtered_df = df.filter("Revenue > 10000")
filtered_df.show()

category_revenue = df.groupBy("Category").sum("Revenue").withColumnRenamed("sum(Revenue)", "CategoryRevenue")
category_revenue.show()


overall_revenue = df.agg({"Revenue": "sum"}).collect()[0][0]

top_category = category_revenue.withColumn(
    "Percentage", (col("CategoryRevenue") / overall_revenue) * 100
).orderBy("Percentage", ascending=False).first()

print(f"Top Category: {top_category['Category']} ({top_category['Percentage']:.2f}% of overall revenue)\n")

df.write.mode("overwrite").option("header", "true").csv(hdfs_output_path)
print(f"Transformed dataset successfully saved to: {hdfs_output_path}")

spark.stop()

4) 4. An e-commerce company maintains two datasets in HDFS:
Orders.csv:
OrderID, CustomerID, ProductID, Quantity, OrderDate
Products.csv:
ProductID, ProductName, Category, UnitPrice
Develop a Spark application to:
Load both datasets into DataFrames.
Join the datasets using ProductID.
Compute the revenue generated for each order.
Determine the monthly revenue trend.
Identify the top 5 products and top 5 customers based on total revenue.
Find the category with the highest sales growth between consecutive months.
Calculate the percentage contribution of each category to total revenue.
Store the final analytical reports in Parquet format on HDFS.


q4.py:

from pyspark.sql import SparkSession
from pyspark.sql.functions import col, date_format, lag, round
from pyspark.sql.window import Window

spark = SparkSession.builder.appName("Ecommerce Analytics").getOrCreate()

base_path = "hdfs://localhost:9000/user/240968040/lab8"

orders = spark.read.option("header", True).option("inferSchema", True).csv(f"{base_path}/Orders.csv")
products = spark.read.option("header", True).option("inferSchema", True).csv(f"{base_path}/Products.csv")

print("--- Orders ---")
orders.show(3)
print("--- Products ---")
products.show(3)

joined_df = orders.join(products, "ProductID").withColumn("Revenue", col("Quantity") * col("UnitPrice")).withColumn("Month", date_format(col("OrderDate"), "yyyy-MM"))

print("--- Joined Dataset with Revenue ---")
joined_df.show(3)

monthly_revenue = joined_df.groupBy("Month").sum("Revenue").withColumnRenamed("sum(Revenue)", "MonthlyRevenue").orderBy("Month")

print("--- Monthly Revenue Trend ---")
monthly_revenue.show()


top_products = joined_df.groupBy("ProductID", "ProductName").sum("Revenue").withColumnRenamed("sum(Revenue)", "TotalRevenue").orderBy(col("TotalRevenue").desc())

print("--- Top 5 Products ---")
top_products.show(5, truncate=False)

top_customers = joined_df.groupBy("CustomerID").sum("Revenue").withColumnRenamed("sum(Revenue)", "TotalRevenue").orderBy(col("TotalRevenue").desc())

print("--- Top 5 Customers ---")
top_customers.show(5)


category_month = joined_df.groupBy("Category", "Month").sum("Revenue").withColumnRenamed("sum(Revenue)", "Revenue")

window_spec = Window.partitionBy("Category").orderBy("Month")
category_growth = category_month.withColumn("PreviousRevenue", lag("Revenue").over(window_spec)).withColumn("Growth", col("Revenue") - col("PreviousRevenue"))

highest_growth = category_growth.filter(col("Growth").isNotNull()).orderBy(col("Growth").desc()).first()

if highest_growth:
    print(f"Highest Growth Category: {highest_growth['Category']} in {highest_growth['Month']} (Growth: {highest_growth['Growth']})\n")


overall_revenue = joined_df.agg({"Revenue": "sum"}).collect()[0][0]

category_percentage = joined_df.groupBy("Category").sum("Revenue").withColumnRenamed("sum(Revenue)", "CategoryRevenue").withColumn("PercentageContribution", round(col("CategoryRevenue") / overall_revenue * 100, 2)).orderBy(col("PercentageContribution").desc())

print("--- Category Percentage Contribution ---")
category_percentage.show(truncate=False)

reports = {
    "monthly_revenue": monthly_revenue,
    "top_products": top_products,
    "top_customers": top_customers,
    "category_growth": category_growth,
    "category_percentage": category_percentage
}

for name, df in reports.items():
    output_path = f"{base_path}/output/q4/{name}"
    df.write.mode("overwrite").parquet(output_path)
    print(f"Saved {name} to {output_path}")

spark.stop()
